# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-21 21:45:23: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-21 21:48:58: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-21 21:48:58: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 07:41:47: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, GET banks at 2025-08-22 07:58:00: 
SELECT banks.id, banks.name, banks.created_at, banks.bank_type_id, bank_types.name as bank_type_name, banks.deleted_at, banks.foto FROM banks  INNER JOIN bank_types ON banks.bank_type_id = bank_types.id WHERE banks.deleted_at IS NULL   ORDER BY banks.name DESC  LIMIT 30 OFFSET 0  
# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 07:58:11: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 07:58:11: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 08:55:56: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 08:55:56: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:00:45: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:00:45: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:05:06: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:05:06: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:06:15: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:06:15: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:06:46: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:06:46: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:11:14: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:11:14: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:17:14: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:17:14: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:18:10: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:18:10: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,

        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:26:30: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:26:30: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:42:45: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:47:23: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:51:16: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:51:16: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 09:52:05: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 09:52:05: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 10:49:15: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 10:51:01: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 10:51:01: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 10:51:34: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 10:51:34: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 10:52:36: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,

        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 10:53:22: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 10:53:22: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 10:58:10: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 10:58:10: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 10:58:34: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 10:58:34: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 14:40:46: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 14:40:46: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_users at 2025-08-22 14:42:19: 
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 14:42:19: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_users (
       id             BIGINT signed AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
       email          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
       phone          VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL,
       password       LONGTEXT NOT NULL,
       name           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
       photo          VARCHAR(50) NULL,
       email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
       enabled        BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NULL,

       created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at    DATETIME null,
       deleted_at    DATETIME null,
       created_by_id bigint signed null,
       updated_by_id bigint signed null,
       deleted_by_id bigint signed null,

       CONSTRAINT idx_user_email UNIQUE (email),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_phone UNIQUE (phone),
       CONSTRAINT idx_user_enabled (enabled)
);

# QUERY, POST generate/table/flx_roles at 2025-08-22 14:43:31: 
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flx_roles (
        id            BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        id_users      BIGINT NOT NULL,
        endpoint      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        role          TINYINT NOT NULL,

        created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at    DATETIME null,
        deleted_at    DATETIME null,
        created_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        updated_by_id bigint unsigned null,
        deleted_by_id bigint unsigned null,

        CONSTRAINT idx_role_name UNIQUE (id_users, endpoint)
    );
